function closeMenu(){document.getElementById('menu-drop').classList.remove('on');}
function openMenu(name,btn){
  const drop=document.getElementById('menu-drop');
  const items=MENUS[name]||[];

/* ---- FS API ---- */
async function fsApi(cmd,path,path2){
  const body={cmd};
  if(path!=null) body.path=path;
  if(path2!=null) body.path2=path2;
  const r=await fetch(BASE+'/api/fs',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});
  const t=await r.text();
  if(!r.ok) throw new Error(t);
  return t;
async function ctrl(o){
  const r=await fetch(BASE+'/api/ctrl',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(o)});
  const t=await r.text(); msg(t,r.ok); return r.ok;
async function optSet(o){
  const r=await fetch(BASE+'/api/opt',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(o)});
  const t=await r.text(); msg(t,r.ok); pollStatus(); return r.ok;
async function setMachine(id){
  await fetch(BASE+'/api/machine',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({id})});
  await optSet({defmachine:id});
  msg('Machine '+id+': '+MACHINES[id],true);
function msg(t,ok){const el=document.getElementById('fs-msg');el.textContent=t;el.className='msg'+(ok===false?' err':ok?' ok':'');}
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));}
function joinPath(dir,name){
  let p=dir||'0:/'; if(!p.endsWith('/')) p+='/';
  return p+name;
function selectedEntry(){return (sel>=0 && entries[sel])?entries[sel]:null;}
function selectedPaths(){
  if(tags.size) return [...tags];
  const e=selectedEntry();
  return e && e.name!=='..' ? [e.full] : [];
async function listDir(path){
  if(path==null) path=document.getElementById('path').value||'0:/';
  if(!path.endsWith('/') && path!=='0:') path+='/';
  if(path==='0:') path='0:/';
  curPath=path; document.getElementById('path').value=path;
  const box=document.getElementById('files');
  listBusy=true; document.getElementById('pill-fs').textContent='fs busy'; document.getElementById('pill-fs').className='pill hot';
    const r=await fetch(BASE+'/api/fs/list?path='+encodeURIComponent(path),{cache:'no-store'});
    const t=await r.text();
    if(!r.ok) throw new Error(t);
    const lines=t.trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean);
    entries=[];
    if(path!=='0:/') entries.push({name:'..',size:0,kind:'D',full:path,ext:''});
    lines.forEach(line=>{
      if(line.startsWith('...')) return;
      const [name,size,kind]=line.split('\t');
      if(!name) return;
      const isDir=kind==='D';
      const ext=isDir?'':(name.includes('.')?name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase():'');
      entries.push({name,size:+size||0,kind:isDir?'D':'F',full:joinPath(path,name)+(isDir?'/':''),ext});
    sortEntries();
    renderFiles();
    msg(entries.length+' entries 
 loader FS mailbox',true);
    document.getElementById('pill-fs').textContent='fs ok';
    document.getElementById('pill-fs').className='pill ok';
  }catch(e){
    entries=[]; renderFiles();
    msg(String(e.message||e),false);
    document.getElementById('pill-fs').textContent='fs offline';
    document.getElementById('pill-fs').className='pill err';
  }finally{listBusy=false;}
function setSort(k){
  if(sortKey===k) sortDir=-sortDir; else {sortKey=k;sortDir=1;}
  sortEntries(); renderFiles();
function sortEntries(){
  const head=entries[0]&&entries[0].name==='..'?entries.shift():null;
  entries.sort((a,b)=>{
    if(a.kind!==b.kind) return a.kind==='D'?-1:1;
    let c=0;
    if(sortKey==='size') c=a.size-b.size;
    else if(sortKey==='kind') c=a.ext.localeCompare(b.ext);
    else c=a.name.localeCompare(b.name,{sensitivity:'base'});
    return c*sortDir;
  if(head) entries.unshift(head);
  document.querySelectorAll('#fs-head span').forEach(s=>{
    const on=s.dataset.s===sortKey;
    s.textContent=s.dataset.s[0].toUpperCase()+s.dataset.s.slice(1)+(on?(sortDir>0?' 
function renderFiles(){
  const box=document.getElementById('files');
  const head=document.getElementById('fs-head');
  box.innerHTML=''; box.appendChild(head);
  if(!entries.length){const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='empty';e.textContent='(empty or offline)';box.appendChild(e);sel=-1;return;}
  if(sel<0||sel>=entries.length) sel=0;
  entries.forEach((e,i)=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.className='f'+(e.kind==='D'?' d':'')+(i===sel?' sel':'')+(tags.has(e.full)?' tag':'');
    const sz=e.kind==='D'?'&lt;DIR&gt;':fmtSize(e.size);
    el.innerHTML=`<span class="n">${e.kind==='D'?'
'} ${esc(e.name)}</span><span>${sz}</span><span>${e.kind}</span>`;
    el.onclick=()=>{setZone('files');sel=i;renderFiles();};
    el.ondblclick=()=>{setZone('files');sel=i;fsActivate();};
    box.appendChild(el);
  const cur=box.querySelector('.f.sel'); if(cur) cur.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});
function fmtSize(n){n=+n||0;if(n<1024)return n+'';if(n<1048576)return (n/1024).toFixed(n<10240?1:0)+'K';return (n/1048576).toFixed(1)+'M';}
function goUp(){
  let p=curPath.replace(/\/+$/,'');
  const i=p.lastIndexOf('/');
  listDir(i<=1?'0:/':p.slice(0,i+1));
async function fsActivate(){
  const e=selectedEntry(); if(!e) return;
  if(e.name==='..'||e.kind==='D'){ if(e.name==='..') goUp(); else listDir(e.full); return; }
  /* launch via autotrig 
 loader dispatches by extension */
  const dir=curPath, name=e.name;
    const r=await fetch(BASE+'/api/launch',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({dir,name})});
    msg(await r.text(),r.ok);
    document.getElementById('pill-load').textContent='load '+name;
    document.getElementById('pill-load').className='pill hot';
  }catch(err){msg(String(err),false);}
async function opCopy(){
  const paths=selectedPaths(); if(!paths.length) return msg('nothing selected',false);
  const dest=await modal('Copy to','<label>Destination path</label><input id="m-in">',curPath);
  if(dest==null) return;
  for(const src of paths){
    const base=src.replace(/\/$/,'').split('/').pop();
    let dst=dest;
    if(dst.endsWith('/')||dst.endsWith(':')) dst=dst+base;
    try{msg(await fsApi('copy',src,dst),true);}catch(e){msg(String(e.message||e),false);return;}
  tags.clear(); listDir();
async function opRename(){
  const e=selectedEntry(); if(!e||e.name==='..') return msg('select a file',false);
  const dest=await modal('Rename / Move','<label>New path</label><input id="m-in">',e.full.replace(/\/$/,''));
  if(dest==null) return;
  try{msg(await fsApi('rename',e.full.replace(/\/$/,''),dest),true);tags.clear();listDir();}
  catch(err){msg(String(err.message||err),false);}
async function opMkdir(){
  const name=await modal('Make directory','<label>Path</label><input id="m-in">',joinPath(curPath,'newdir'));
  if(name==null) return;
  try{msg(await fsApi('mkdir',name),true);listDir();}
  catch(err){msg(String(err.message||err),false);}
async function opDelete(){
  const paths=selectedPaths(); if(!paths.length) return msg('nothing selected',false);
  const ok=await modal('Delete','<p>Delete '+paths.length+' item(s)? Recursive for folders.</p><input type="hidden" value="1">','1');
  if(ok==null) return;
  for(const p of paths){
    try{msg(await fsApi('delete',p.replace(/\/$/,'')),true);}catch(e){msg(String(e.message||e),false);return;}
  tags.clear(); listDir();
function toggleTag(){
  const e=selectedEntry(); if(!e||e.name==='..') return;
  if(tags.has(e.full)) tags.delete(e.full); else tags.add(e.full);
  if(sel<entries.length-1) sel++;
  renderFiles();

function resizeFb(w,h,sz,hz){
  W=w|0;H=h|0;FB_SIZE=sz|0; if(hz) targetHz=hz|0;
  cv.width=W;cv.height=H;cv.style.aspectRatio=W+'/'+H;
  img=ctx.createImageData(W,H);pix32=new Uint32Array(img.data.buffer);
  document.getElementById('geom').textContent=W+'
 '+(FB_SIZE>>10)+' KiB 
 '+targetHz+' Hz';
function decode4bpp(u8){
  const need=Math.min(u8.length,(W*H)>>1); let o=0;
  for(let i=0;i<need;i++){const b=u8[i];pix32[o++]=PAL32[b&15];pix32[o++]=PAL32[(b>>4)&15];}
async function fetchFrame(){
  const r=await fetch(BASE+'/frame',{cache:'no-store'});
  if(!r.ok) throw new Error(r.status);
  return {headers:r.headers,buf:new Uint8Array(await r.arrayBuffer())};
async function stream(){
  if(!run) return;
  const t0=performance.now();
    const cur=nextFetch||fetchFrame(); nextFetch=fetchFrame();
    const {headers,buf}=await cur;
    if(!buf.length) throw new Error('empty');
    const hw=+headers.get('X-FB-W'),hh=+headers.get('X-FB-H'),hz=+headers.get('X-FB-HZ');
    const fam=headers.get('X-FB-FAMILY'); if(fam) family=fam;
    if(hw&&hh&&(hw!==W||hh!==H||buf.length!==FB_SIZE)) resizeFb(hw,hh,buf.length,hz||targetHz);
    else if(buf.length===57984&&FB_SIZE!==57984) resizeFb(384,302,57984,hz||50);
    else if(buf.length===30720&&FB_SIZE!==30720) resizeFb(256,240,30720,hz||60);
    decode4bpp(buf); ctx.putImageData(img,0,0);
    frames++; lastMs=performance.now()-t0;
    document.getElementById('lat').textContent=lastMs.toFixed(0)+' ms 
'+H+(fam?' 
 '+fam:'');
    document.getElementById('dot').className='dot on';
    document.getElementById('pill-link').textContent='live';
    document.getElementById('pill-link').className='pill ok';
  }catch(e){
    nextFetch=null;
    document.getElementById('dot').className='dot bad';
    document.getElementById('pill-link').textContent='down';
    document.getElementById('pill-link').className='pill err';
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,250));
  const wait=Math.max(0,1000/(targetHz||50)-(performance.now()-t0));
  if(wait>2) setTimeout(()=>requestAnimationFrame(stream),wait); else requestAnimationFrame(stream);
setInterval(()=>{document.getElementById('fps').textContent=frames+' FPS';frames=0;},1000);

const PS2={KeyA:0x1C,KeyB:0x32,KeyC:0x21,KeyD:0x23,KeyE:0x24,KeyF:0x2B,KeyG:0x34,KeyH:0x33,KeyI:0x43,KeyJ:0x3B,KeyK:0x42,KeyL:0x4B,KeyM:0x3A,KeyN:0x31,KeyO:0x44,KeyP:0x4D,KeyQ:0x15,KeyR:0x2D,KeyS:0x1B,KeyT:0x2C,KeyU:0x3C,KeyV:0x2A,KeyW:0x1D,KeyX:0x22,KeyY:0x35,KeyZ:0x1A,Digit0:0x45,Digit1:0x16,Digit2:0x1E,Digit3:0x26,Digit4:0x25,Digit5:0x2E,Digit6:0x36,Digit7:0x3D,Digit8:0x3E,Digit9:0x46,Space:0x29,Enter:0x5A,Backspace:0x66,Tab:0x0D,Escape:0x76,ShiftLeft:0x12,ShiftRight:0x59,ControlLeft:0x14,AltLeft:0x11,ArrowUp:0x75,ArrowDown:0x72,ArrowLeft:0x6B,ArrowRight:0x74,F1:0x05,F2:0x06,F5:0x03,F6:0x0B,F7:0x83,F8:0x0A,F9:0x01,F10:0x09,F11:0x78,F12:0x07};
const JOY_KEY={ArrowRight:1,ArrowLeft:2,ArrowDown:4,ArrowUp:8,KeyZ:16,KeyX:32,KeyC:64,Space:16};
async function sendKey(code,release){try{await fetch(BASE+'/api/key',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({code,release:release?1:0})});}catch(_){}}
async function sendJoy(mask){joyMask=mask|0;try{await fetch(BASE+'/api/joy',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({mask:joyMask})});}catch(_){}}
function releaseAllGuestKeys(){heldKeys.forEach(c=>sendKey(c,1));heldKeys.clear();if(joyMask)sendJoy(0);}
window.addEventListener('keydown',ev=>{
  if(document.getElementById('modal').classList.contains('on')) return;
  const tag=(ev.target&&ev.target.tagName)||'';
  if(tag==='INPUT'||tag==='SELECT'||tag==='TEXTAREA'){if(zone!=='ui')setZone('ui');return;}
  /* global navigator hotkeys when Files zone (or always for F-keys if not screen) */
  if(zone==='files' || (zone!=='screen' && /^F\d/.test(ev.key))){
    if(ev.key==='ArrowDown'){ev.preventDefault();sel=Math.min(entries.length-1,sel+1);renderFiles();return;}
    if(ev.key==='ArrowUp'){ev.preventDefault();sel=Math.max(0,sel-1);renderFiles();return;}
    if(ev.key==='Home'){ev.preventDefault();sel=0;renderFiles();return;}
    if(ev.key==='End'){ev.preventDefault();sel=entries.length-1;renderFiles();return;}
    if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();fsActivate();return;}
    if(ev.key==='Backspace'){ev.preventDefault();goUp();return;}
    if(ev.key==='Insert'){ev.preventDefault();toggleTag();return;}
    if(ev.key==='Delete'||ev.key==='F8'){ev.preventDefault();opDelete();return;}
    if(ev.key==='F5'){ev.preventDefault();opCopy();return;}
    if(ev.key==='F6'){ev.preventDefault();opRename();return;}
    if(ev.key==='F7'){ev.preventDefault();opMkdir();return;}
    if(ev.key==='F10'){ev.preventDefault();ctrl({halt:1});return;}
    if(ev.key==='F11'){ev.preventDefault();ctrl({reset:1});return;}
    if(ev.key==='F1'){ev.preventDefault();showHelp();return;}
    return;
  if(zone!=='screen') return;
  ev.preventDefault();
  const sc=PS2[ev.code];
  if(sc!=null&&!ev.repeat){heldKeys.add(sc);sendKey(sc,0);}
  const jb=JOY_KEY[ev.code]; if(jb!=null) sendJoy(joyMask|jb);
window.addEventListener('keyup',ev=>{
  if(zone!=='screen') return;
  const sc=PS2[ev.code]; if(sc!=null){heldKeys.delete(sc);sendKey(sc,1);}
  const jb=JOY_KEY[ev.code]; if(jb!=null) sendJoy(joyMask&~jb);
window.addEventListener('blur',()=>releaseAllGuestKeys());
